HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000937/B001","root_000937/B002","root_001132/B001","root_001290/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_09aedb2e38e1193d4c15","text":"1. **Denial as overflow beyond a limit.** Lexical evidence: ك ذ ب B002 \"declaring something or someone false\"; ط غ ي B001 \"overstepping the bound in rebellion\"; ط غ ي B002 \"overflowing and overwhelming force\"; ف ج ر B001/B004 splitting/outflow and breach in 91:8. Structural cue: after the general moral pair, the narrative gives a collective case: Thamud denied by/with its طغوى. Assigned role: طغوى supplies the force that pushes denial past an internal boundary. Abductive move: I infer that denial is not only a statement but a flood-like transgression generated by excess; the packet supplies denial, overstepping, and overflow branches. Reading change: the ayah reads as the social eruption of the inner فجور described earlier.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B004","root_001290/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_22dfa58ded70e5fa68bf","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:11:1\",\"root_001290\",[\"B004\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yalanlama, üstlenilen sorumluluğu boşa çıkarıp gecikmeden şiddete yönelen bir eylem programı olarak işler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000937/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2dc49841c224717378fc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:11:3\",\"root_000937\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Azgınlık, topluluğun kendini üstün sandığı fakat düşüşe açık, yüksek ve kaygan bir kaya gibi tasvir edilebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_392e1cf3fa68a9daf174","text":"2. **Takdhib as falsifying the messenger and the protected sign.** Lexical evidence: ك ذ ب B002 \"declaring false\"; ر س ل B002 \"messenger and message\" and ء ل ه B002 in 91:13; ن و ق B002 she-camel; س ق ي B001/B002 watering right. Structural cue: 91:11 introduces denial before 91:13 identifies messenger, she-camel of Allah, and watering. Assigned role: كذب is activated by the later object it rejects: messenger/sign/right. Abductive move: I supply the referential arrow from this denial to the later messenger and she-camel; the packet supplies the sequence. Reading change: كذبت ثمود becomes an anticipatory legal-social breach: they are already positioned against a sent claim and a protected creature.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B004","root_001290/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4c2d808842070c06c8c4","text":"3. **Falsehood as failed charge, later enacted bodily.** Lexical evidence: ك ذ ب B004 \"fulfilling or failing a charge\"; ك ذ ب B005 \"not delaying to act\"; ع ق ر B002 \"cutting an animal down\" in 91:14. Structural cue: verbal denial in 91:11 escalates into physical hamstringing in 91:14. Assigned role: the \"charge\" branch makes takdhib an action that either proves itself in motion or fails morally. Abductive move: I infer that their denial becomes performative: they make their false stance true as aggression by not delaying into action; the packet supplies denial/charge/no-delay and hamstringing. Reading change: the ayah is not merely a report of disbelief; it is the ignition of a chain where speech hardens into deed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000937/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_656dcb67fbfb1f5222a1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:11:3\",\"root_000937\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Azgınlık sahte bir otoriteye dönüştüğünde, topluluktan çıkan en kötü kişi ilahi bildirime karşı işleyen alternatif bir buyruğun uygulayıcısı olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000808/B004","root_000937/B005","root_001033/B012"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_74413040c97de0b95efb","text":"4. **Smooth high rock branch makes transgression spatial.** Lexical evidence: ط غ ي B005 \"smooth rock or high outcrop\"; ش ق و B004 \"long rising mountain flank\" in 91:12; ع ق ب B012 \"steep pass and jutting rock\" in 91:15. Structural cue: طغوى is followed by أشقىها and final عقبها, both with mountain/pass branches. Assigned role: ṭughwa's outcrop gives rebellion a raised, hard surface. Abductive move: I infer a terrain-image for moral excess: the group has climbed onto a high exposed transgressive stance. Reading change: \"by its طغوى\" gains a topographical feel, as if their rebellion is a dangerous elevation from which the wretched one is roused.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7d690dca13fe2ccafe68","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:11:1\",\"root_001290\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Deve sütünün beklenen süre boyunca gelmemesi, topluluğun ileride hayvanı öldürerek sürdürülebilir nimeti fiilen kesmesini yalanlamanın maddi sonucu hâline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d297b00c221255f30702","text":"91:13–14 supplied the object of the denial, so 91:11 changed from a general collective moral diagnosis into the opening of a legal case: messenger, divine she-camel, and watering right are what their طغوى overruns. 91:15 then added that the overstepping creates an aftermath no one can appeal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000937/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_da136b18e72081ab7190","text":"4. **Rebellion as an alternative command structure.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ط غ ي/B003` is a false authority or head of deviation; 91:12 raises `أشقاها`, while 91:13 repeats `ٱللَّهِ` around the protected she-camel. **Structural cue and role:** a superlative individual emerges from the collective just before divine ownership is announced, giving the false-authority branch a social role opposed to the messenger's command. **Abductive move:** the text does not title the individual a leader; construing him as the operative head of collective deviation is inferred from sequence and action. **Reading change:** `طغواها` includes a rival regime of obedience, not merely excess appetite.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B003","root_000937/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_dfedc8f7e22a6bf69b67","text":"2. **A water-boundary overrun.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ط غ ي/B002` is water or another force rising beyond measure and overwhelming; 91:13 names the she-camel's watering through `س ق ي/B003`, an irrigation share, and 91:14 brings eradication through `د م د م/B001`. **Structural cue and role:** transgression is followed by an allocated water relation, its violation, and an overwhelming counter-event, so overflow functions as seizure beyond one's share. **Abductive move:** the water share is explicit, but reading `طغواها` materially as resource overrun is inferred from the branch. **Reading change:** Thamud's rebellion becomes a concrete failure to remain within a life-sustaining allocation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000937/B001","root_000937/B002","root_001290/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e2b464b5fd1204733514","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:11:1\",\"root_001290\",[\"B002\"]],[\"91:11:3\",\"root_000937\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Azgınlık, **Transgressive overflow producing enacted denial.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ك ذ ب/B002` is declaring a person or report false and fixed `ط غ ي/B001` is overstepping a bound in rebellion. **Structural cue and role:** `بطغواها` grammatically attaches transgression to the denial; the warning is voiced in 91:13 and `فكذبوه فعقروها` in 91:14 repeats denial before bodily action. **Abductive move:** attachment and repetition are supplied; the arrow \"habitual overstepping makes the warning intolerable and therefore denied\" is inferred. **Reading change:** `كذبت` is not an isolated opinion but the epistemic operation by which rebellion protects itself and becomes action.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fb5277e6cfe3d6866683","text":"3. dormant image of milk and continuous yield, before 91:14 disables her. **Structural cue and role:** the generic denial in 91:11 is retrospectively given an animal and a sustaining flow, then the community itself makes that flow fail. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies the rare milk branch and later she-camel; construing human violence as causatively realizing the expectation-failure is inferred. **Reading change:** denial becomes the practical destruction of a promised provision, not only rejection of a
